API

#ui/widgets

Xu {{xu_version}} API

import: #ui/widgets

counts: 62 fns, 989 methods, 46 types, 34 consts, 4 ifaces

Button, Checkbox, Progress, Scroll, Input / TextArea and retained View. Styles provides fonts, density, palettes, geometry and combined-state rules; StyledLabel caches layout. Local themes are complete snapshots. Register measurement with set_style_measure, call mark_measure after text changes, then layout_styled, place_editor and present_styled. Callbacks borrow themes, use node-local coordinates and must not mutate View or topology.

Usage and lifetimes

Extended controls and style switching

Switch, Radio, Slider, Stepper, Tabs, Select, Badge, Divider, Card and ListBox share Styles, combined-state paint rules, shadows and icon placement. Create widgets.style_switcher() to select dark, light or contrast presets. Use use_preset, set_text_scale and set_density, then apply to the retained View; cycle advances and applies a preset. These methods can fail, so handle their errors. Each View receives a complete style snapshot; explicit control overrides remain in effect.

Release examples (complete projects and source downloads): widgets · editor_workbench · style_gallery · controls_gallery

Apply shared styles to a retained View:

@main
#ui/widgets

fn configure(view &mut widgets.View) int {
    mut themes := widgets.style_switcher()
    themes.use_preset(widgets.theme_light) or >>
    themes.set_text_scale(1.2) or >>
    themes.set_density(0.85) or >>
    themes.apply(view) or >>
    // Call this from a theme-switch action to advance and apply the preset.
    themes.cycle(view) or >>
    << 0
}
fn main() int { << 0 }

Examples · API declarations

Examples

These sources come from the official examples repository and can be copied or downloaded. Run them with a local Xu SDK in the complete project; network, database, and desktop examples require the corresponding services or assets.

16_ui_webview/controls_gallery/main.xu

Complete project · Download source

XuLocal SDK example
@main
#ui
#ui/draw
#ui/layout
#ui/widgets
demo #"github.com/xulang-org/examples/ui_demo"

fn rect(x f64,y f64,width f64,height f64) layout.Rect {
    << layout.Rect{x: x,y: y,width: width,height: height}
}

// One worker owns the controls and their canvas resources. Every recorded
// label remains alive until the complete frame has been accepted.
fn scene(ech chan ui.Event) int {
    ready := demo.ready(ech) or >>
    if ready.kind == ui.ev_exit { << 0 }
    identity := ui.canvas_of(&ready) or >>
    context := draw.context_of(&ready) or >>
    mut switcher := widgets.style_switcher()
    switcher.use_preset(widgets.theme_light) or >>
    mut toggle := widgets.switch_control(&identity,1,&rect(48.0,116.0,280.0,48.0),true) or >>
    mut radio := widgets.radio(&identity,2,&rect(48.0,180.0,280.0,48.0),1,false) or >>
    mut slider := widgets.slider(&identity,3,&rect(48.0,260.0,280.0,36.0),0.0,100.0,50.0) or >>
    slider.set_step(5.0) or >>
    mut stepper := widgets.stepper(&identity,4,&rect(48.0,324.0,280.0,48.0),0.0,20.0,1.0,5.0) or >>
    mut tabs := widgets.tabs(&identity,5,&rect(376.0,116.0,348.0,48.0),3,0) or >>
    mut dropdown := widgets.select_box(&identity,6,&rect(376.0,196.0,348.0,48.0),3,0) or >>
    mut list := widgets.list_box(&identity,7,&rect(376.0,284.0,348.0,132.0),3,0) or >>
    panel := widgets.card(&rect(24.0,92.0,724.0,352.0)) or >>
    badge := widgets.badge(&rect(48.0,392.0,280.0,32.0)) or >>
    divider := widgets.divider(&rect(48.0,244.0,280.0,2.0),widgets.axis_horizontal) or >>
    mut label0 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Enabled")
    mut label1 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Choose this option")
    mut label2 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Stepper")
    mut label3 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"One")
    mut label4 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Two")
    mut label5 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Three")
    mut label6 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Select an option")
    mut label7 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Xu 0.10.1")
    mut label8 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"One")
    mut label9 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Two")
    mut label10 := widgets.styled_label(&context,&"Three")
    mut frame := context.new_frame(16384)
    mut dirty := true
    mut focus := 0
    while true {
        if dirty {
            styles := switcher.styles()
            frame.begin() or >>
            frame.set_bg(styles.background) or >>
            panel.paint_styled(mut &frame,&styles) or >>
            ink := styles.button.palette.foreground
            title := "Extended controls / " + switcher.name()
            frame.text(32.0,28.0,22.0,ink,&title) or >>
            toggle.paint_styled(mut &frame,mut &label0,&styles) or >>
            radio.paint_styled(mut &frame,mut &label1,&styles) or >>
            divider.paint_styled(mut &frame,&styles) or >>
            slider.paint_styled(mut &frame,&styles) or >>
            stepper.paint_styled(mut &frame,mut &label2,&styles) or >>
            tabs.paint_box_styled(mut &frame,&styles) or >>
            list.paint_box_styled(mut &frame,&styles) or >>
            tabs.paint_tab_styled(mut &frame,0,mut &label3,&styles) or >>
            list.paint_item_styled(mut &frame,0,mut &label8,&styles) or >>
            tabs.paint_tab_styled(mut &frame,1,mut &label4,&styles) or >>
            list.paint_item_styled(mut &frame,1,mut &label9,&styles) or >>
            tabs.paint_tab_styled(mut &frame,2,mut &label5,&styles) or >>
            list.paint_item_styled(mut &frame,2,mut &label10,&styles) or >>
            dropdown.paint_styled(mut &frame,mut &label6,&styles) or >>
            badge.paint_styled(mut &frame,mut &label7,&styles) or >>
            frame.text(32.0,464.0,15.0,ink,&"T: theme / S: text size / D: density / Tab: focus / Esc: quit") or >>
            status := "Slider " + str(int(slider.value())) + " / Stepper " + str(int(stepper.value())) + " / Select " + str(dropdown.selected() + 1)
            frame.text(32.0,494.0,15.0,ink,&status) or >>
            // A Select popup overlays the scene; this basic painter repeats
            // its label. The selected option index is shown in the status.
            dropdown.paint_popup_styled(mut &frame,mut &label6,&styles) or >>
            frame.present() or >>
            toggle.painted()
            radio.painted()
            slider.painted()
            stepper.painted()
            tabs.painted()
            dropdown.painted()
            list.painted()
            dirty = false
        }
        event := demo.next(ech)
        if event.kind == ui.ev_exit { <- }
        if event.window_id != identity.window_id { -> }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_canvas_closed { <- }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_canvas_metrics { dirty = true }
        if ui.is_input_event(event.kind) {
            input := ui.input_of(&event) or >>
            if input.generation != identity.generation { -> }
            if input.kind == ui.ev_key_down && input.repeat == 0 {
                if input.key == ui.key_escape {
                    // App shutdown revokes canvas resources. Do not enqueue
                    // another frame while the native host is closing.
                    demo.quit() or >>
                    << 0
                }
                if input.key == 84 || input.key == 116 { switcher.next() or >>
                    dirty = true }
                if input.key == 83 || input.key == 115 {
                    mut scale := 1.4
                    if switcher.text_scale() > 1.0 { scale = 1.0 }
                    switcher.set_text_scale(scale) or >>
                    dirty = true
                }
                if input.key == 68 || input.key == 100 {
                    mut density := 0.85
                    if switcher.density() < 1.0 { density = 1.0 }
                    switcher.set_density(density) or >>
                    dirty = true
                }
                if input.key == ui.key_tab { focus = focus % 7 + 1
                    dirty = true }
            }
        }
        // Route the popup first. A modal option click must not also focus
        // or activate the ListBox underneath it.
        popup_open := dropdown.is_open()
        first := dropdown.event(&event) or >>
        mut actions := [first]
        if !popup_open && !first.consumed {
            actions.push(toggle.event(&event) or >>)
            actions.push(radio.event(&event) or >>)
            actions.push(slider.event(&event) or >>)
            actions.push(stepper.event(&event) or >>)
            actions.push(tabs.event(&event) or >>)
            actions.push(list.event(&event) or >>)
        }
        for action in actions {
            if action.request_focus { focus = action.id }
            if action.needs_paint { dirty = true }
        }
        toggle.set_focus(focus == 1)
        radio.set_focus(focus == 2)
        slider.set_focus(focus == 3)
        stepper.set_focus(focus == 4)
        tabs.set_focus(focus == 5)
        dropdown.set_focus(focus == 6)
        list.set_focus(focus == 7)
    }
    frame.dispose()
    if demo.alive(&identity) {
        label0.dispose()
        label1.dispose()
        label2.dispose()
        label3.dispose()
        label4.dispose()
        label5.dispose()
        label6.dispose()
        label7.dispose()
        label8.dispose()
        label9.dispose()
        label10.dispose()
    }
    << 0
}
fn worker(ech chan ui.Event) {
    scene(ech) or (err) { demo.fail(err.msg())
        << }
}
fn launch() int {
    mut app := demo.app("Xu UI / Extended Controls",780,552) or >>
    xu worker(app.event_ch)
    << app.run() or >>
}
fn main() int {
    << launch() or (err) { eprintln(err.msg())
        << 1 }
}
16_ui_webview/editor_workbench/main.xu

Complete project · Download source

XuLocal SDK example
@main
#ui
#ui/draw
#ui/widgets
#ui/layout
demo #"github.com/xulang-org/examples/ui_demo"

struct Model {
    title widgets.Editor
    body widgets.Editor
    context draw.Context
    theme widgets.Theme
    header int
    title_id int
    body_id int
    footer int
    changes int
    status string
}
fn (model &mut Model) input(id int,kind int,event &ui.InputEvent) widgets.Action {
    if id == model.title_id { control := mut &model.title
        << control.route(kind,event) or >> }
    if id == model.body_id { control := mut &model.body
        << control.route(kind,event) or >> }
    << widgets.Action{kind: 0,id: id,needs_paint: false,request_focus: false,consumed: false,value: 0.0}
}
fn (model &mut Model) paint(id int,bounds &layout.Rect,frame &mut draw.Frame) bool {
    model.draw_node(id,frame) or (err) { eprintln(err.msg())
        << false }
    << true
}
fn (model &mut Model) draw_node(id int,frame &mut draw.Frame) int {
    theme := model.theme
    if id == model.title_id { control := mut &model.title
        control.paint(frame,&theme) or >> }
    else if id == model.body_id { control := mut &model.body
        control.paint(frame,&theme) or >> }
    else if id == model.header {
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,0.0,14.0,demo.accent(),"08 / INPUT + TEXTAREA + DESKTOP") or >>
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,27.0,24.0,demo.ink(),"A small native writing desk") or >>
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,62.0,13.0,demo.muted(),"Tab / Shift+Tab · drag selection · copy / cut / paste · arrows / Home / End") or >>
    } else if id == model.footer {
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,0.0,13.0,demo.accent(),"Changes: " + str(model.changes) + "  |  " + model.status) or >>
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,27.0,12.0,demo.muted(),"Use the Document menu: toggle editing, reset, query screens. Escape quits.") or >>
    }
    << 0
}
fn (model &mut Model) place(view &mut widgets.View) int {
    title := mut &model.title
    widgets.place_editor(view,title) or >>
    body := mut &model.body
    widgets.place_editor(view,body) or >>
    << 0
}
fn (model &mut Model) native_event(event &ui.Event) bool {
    title := mut &model.title
    first := title.event(event) or >>
    body := mut &model.body
    second := body.event(event) or >>
    << first.kind == widgets.action_change || second.kind == widgets.action_change
}
fn (model &mut Model) editing(on bool) int {
    title := mut &model.title
    title.enable(on) or >>
    body := mut &model.body
    body.enable(on) or >>
    << 0
}
fn (model &mut Model) reset() int {
    title := mut &model.title
    title.set_value(&"Untitled note") or >>
    body := mut &model.body
    body.set_value(&"Write here.\n你好，Xu。\n\nSelection and IME are native; layout and painting stay in the retained View.") or >>
    << 0
}
fn menus(window &ui.Window,enabled bool) int {
    mut entries := [ui.submenu("document","Document",-1)]
    mut reset := ui.menu_action("reset","Reset note",0)
    reset.key = 82
    reset.modifiers = ui.menu_primary | ui.mod_shift
    entries.push(reset)
    entries.push(ui.menu_check("editing","Enable editing",0,enabled))
    entries.push(ui.menu_separator(0))
    entries.push(ui.submenu("display","Displays",0))
    mut query := ui.menu_action("screens","Query current screens",4)
    query.key = 68
    query.modifiers = ui.menu_primary | ui.mod_shift
    entries.push(query)
    mut unavailable := ui.menu_action("unavailable","Unavailable action",4)
    unavailable.enabled = false
    entries.push(unavailable)
    << window.set_menu_entries(entries) or >>
}
fn scene(ech chan ui.Event) int {
    ready := demo.ready(ech) or >>
    if ready.kind == ui.ev_exit { << 0 }
    identity := ui.canvas_of(&ready) or >>
    context := draw.context_of(&ready) or >>
    window := ui.Window{id: identity.window_id,generation: identity.generation,title: "",width: 0,height: 0,content_kind: ui.content_draw,content_backend_id: identity.canvas_id,closed: 0}
    menus(&window,true) or >>
    host := widgets.Host{context: context,identity: identity}
    mut column := layout.box_style(layout.box_column)
    column.padding = layout.insets(24.0) or >>
    column.gap = 16.0
    column.cross_alignment = layout.cross_stretch
    mut view := host.view(&column,demo.background()) or >>
    leaf := layout.box_style(layout.box_leaf)
    header := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 500.0,height: 88.0}) or >>
    title_id := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 500.0,height: 44.0}) or >>
    body_id := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 500.0,height: 260.0}) or >>
    footer := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 500.0,height: 72.0}) or >>
    for id in [header,title_id,body_id,footer] { view.set_boundary(id,true) or >> }
    for id in [title_id,body_id] { view.set_input(id,true,true) or >> }
    empty := layout.Rect{x: 0.0,y: 0.0,width: 0.0,height: 0.0}
    mut model := Model{title: widgets.input(&context,&identity,title_id,&empty,&"Untitled note") or >>,
        body: widgets.text_area(&context,&identity,body_id,&empty,&"Write here.\n你好，Xu。\n\nSelection and IME are native; layout and painting stay in the retained View.") or >>,
        context: context,theme: widgets.default_theme(),header: header,title_id: title_id,body_id: body_id,footer: footer,changes: 0,status: "Ready"}
    mut width := 820.0
    mut height := 620.0
    mut enabled := true
    options := ui.RequestOptions{timeout_ms: 5000}
    mut screens := window.screens_async(&options) or >>
    while true {
        constraints := layout.tight(width,height) or >>
        _ := view.layout(&constraints) or >>
        model.place(mut &view) or >>
        submitted := widgets.present(mut &view,&constraints,mut &model) or >>
        if submitted {
            first := mut &model.title
            first.painted()
            second := mut &model.body
            second.painted()
        }
        event := demo.next(ech)
        if event.kind == ui.ev_exit { <- }
        if event.window_id != identity.window_id { -> }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_canvas_metrics { metrics := ui.metrics_of(&event) or >>
            width = metrics.width
            height = metrics.height }
        actions := widgets.dispatch_event(mut &view,&event,mut &model) or >>
        if model.native_event(&event) or >> { model.changes = model.changes + 1
            view.mark_paint(footer) or >> }
        for action in actions { if action.kind == widgets.action_submit { model.status = "Title submitted: " + model.title.value()
            view.mark_paint(footer) or >> } }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_menu_clicked {
            if event.menu_id == "reset" { model.reset() or >> }
            if event.menu_id == "editing" { enabled = !enabled
                model.editing(enabled) or >>
                for id in [title_id,body_id] { view.set_input(id,enabled,enabled) or >> }
                menus(&window,enabled) or >> }
            if event.menu_id == "screens" { screens.cancel()
                screens = window.screens_async(&options) or >> }
        }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_request { result := ui.request_of(&event) or >>
            if result.matches(&screens) && result.status == ui.request_completed {
                displays := ui.screen_result(&result) or >>
                model.status = str(displays.len) + " display(s)"
                for display in displays { println("Screen: " + display.name + " / " + str(int(display.bounds.width)) + " x " + str(int(display.bounds.height)) + " / scale " + str(int(display.scale_x)))
                    if display.current { model.status = display.name + " · " + str(int(display.bounds.width)) + " x " + str(int(display.bounds.height)) } }
                view.mark_paint(footer) or >>
            }
        }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_canvas_closed || event.kind == ui.ev_closed { <- }
        if ui.is_input_event(event.kind) { input := ui.input_of(&event) or >>
            if input.kind == ui.ev_key_down && input.key == ui.key_escape { demo.quit() or >> } }
    }
    screens.cancel()
    view.dispose()
    << 0
}
fn worker(ech chan ui.Event) { scene(ech) or (err) { demo.fail(err.msg())
    << } }
fn main() int {
    mut app := demo.app("Xu UI / Writing Desk",820,620) or (err) { eprintln(err.msg())
        << 1 }
    xu worker(app.event_ch)
    << app.run() or (err) { eprintln(err.msg())
        << 1 }
}
16_ui_webview/widgets/main.xu

Complete project · Download source

XuLocal SDK example
@main
#ui
#ui/draw
#ui/layout
#ui/widgets
demo #"github.com/xulang-org/examples/ui_demo"

struct Model {
    button widgets.Button
    checkbox widgets.Checkbox
    progress widgets.Progress
    scroll widgets.Scroll
    button_label draw.TextLayout
    checkbox_label draw.TextLayout
    theme widgets.Theme
    context draw.Context
    count int
    header_id int
    button_id int
    check_id int
    progress_id int
    scroll_id int
    row_ids []int
}
fn (model &mut Model) button_input(kind int,input &ui.InputEvent) widgets.Action {
    button := mut &model.button
    << button.route(kind,input) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) checkbox_input(kind int,input &ui.InputEvent) widgets.Action {
    checkbox := mut &model.checkbox
    << checkbox.route(kind,input) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) scroll_input(kind int,input &ui.InputEvent) widgets.Action {
    scroll := mut &model.scroll
    << scroll.route(kind,input) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) input(id int,kind int,input &ui.InputEvent) widgets.Action {
    if id == model.button_id { << model.button_input(kind,input) or >> }
    if id == model.check_id { << model.checkbox_input(kind,input) or >> }
    if id == model.scroll_id { << model.scroll_input(kind,input) or >> }
    << widgets.Action{kind: widgets.action_none,id: id,needs_paint: false,request_focus: false,consumed: false,value: 0.0}
}
fn (model &mut Model) place_button(bounds &layout.Rect) int {
    button := mut &model.button
    << button.set_bounds(bounds) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) place_checkbox(bounds &layout.Rect) int {
    checkbox := mut &model.checkbox
    << checkbox.set_bounds(bounds) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) place_progress(bounds &layout.Rect) int {
    progress := mut &model.progress
    << progress.set_bounds(bounds) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) paint(id int,bounds &layout.Rect,frame &mut draw.Frame) bool {
    model.draw_node(id,bounds,frame) or (err) { eprintln(err.msg())
        << false }
    << true
}
fn (model &mut Model) draw_node(id int,bounds &layout.Rect,frame &mut draw.Frame) int {
    if id == model.header_id {
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,0.0,14.0,demo.accent(),"03 / RETAINED LAYOUT + CONTROLS") or >>
        demo.text(&model.context,frame,0.0,28.0,24.0,demo.ink(),"Actions completed: " + str(model.count)) or >>
    } else if id == model.button_id {
        model.place_button(bounds) or >>
        model.button.paint(frame,&model.button_label,&model.theme) or >>
    } else if id == model.check_id {
        model.place_checkbox(bounds) or >>
        model.checkbox.paint(frame,&model.checkbox_label,&model.theme) or >>
    } else if id == model.progress_id {
        model.place_progress(bounds) or >>
        model.progress.paint(frame,&model.theme) or >>
    } else if id == model.scroll_id {
        scroll := mut &model.scroll
        scroll.set_bounds(bounds) or >>
        frame.fill_rect(0.0,0.0,bounds.width,bounds.height,draw.rgb(26,37,50)) or >>
    } else {
        for i in 0:model.row_ids.len {
            if id == model.row_ids[i] { demo.text(&model.context,frame,12.0,5.0,15.0,demo.muted(),"Retained row " + str(i + 1) + " / scroll, Tab, PageDown, Home") or >> }
        }
    }
    << 0
}
fn (model &mut Model) advance() int {
    model.count = model.count + 1
    println("Action count: " + str(model.count))
    value := f64(model.count % 11) / 10.0
    progress := mut &model.progress
    << progress.set_value(value) or >>
}
fn (model &mut Model) enable_button(enabled bool) {
    button := mut &model.button
    button.set_enabled(enabled)
}
fn (model &mut Model) acknowledge_button() {
    control := mut &model.button
    control.painted()
}
fn (model &mut Model) acknowledge_checkbox() {
    control := mut &model.checkbox
    control.painted()
}
fn (model &mut Model) acknowledge_progress() {
    control := mut &model.progress
    control.painted()
}
fn (model &mut Model) acknowledge_scroll() {
    control := mut &model.scroll
    control.painted()
}
fn (model &mut Model) acknowledge() {
    model.acknowledge_button()
    model.acknowledge_checkbox()
    model.acknowledge_progress()
    model.acknowledge_scroll()
}
fn scene(ech chan ui.Event) int {
    ready := demo.ready(ech) or >>
    if ready.kind == ui.ev_exit { << 0 }
    identity := ui.canvas_of(&ready) or >>
    context := draw.context_of(&ready) or >>
    host := widgets.Host{context: context,identity: identity}
    mut column := layout.box_style(layout.box_column)
    column.padding = layout.insets(28.0) or >>
    column.gap = 14.0
    column.cross_alignment = layout.cross_stretch
    mut view := host.view(&column,demo.background()) or >>
    leaf := layout.box_style(layout.box_leaf)
    header := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 400.0,height: 72.0}) or >>
    button_id := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 280.0,height: 44.0}) or >>
    check_id := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 280.0,height: 40.0}) or >>
    progress_id := view.add(view.root(),&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 280.0,height: 12.0}) or >>
    mut viewport := layout.box_style(layout.box_column)
    viewport.height = 220.0
    viewport.clip = true
    viewport.cross_alignment = layout.cross_stretch
    viewport.gap = 6.0
    scroll_id := view.add(view.root(),&viewport,&layout.Size{width: 400.0,height: 220.0}) or >>
    mut rows := []int{}
    for _ in 0:20 {
        row := view.add(scroll_id,&leaf,&layout.Size{width: 400.0,height: 32.0}) or >>
        rows.push(row)
        view.set_boundary(row,true) or >>
    }
    for id in [header,button_id,check_id,progress_id,scroll_id] { view.set_boundary(id,true) or >> }
    for id in [button_id,check_id,scroll_id] { view.set_input(id,true,true) or >> }
    empty := layout.Rect{x: 0.0,y: 0.0,width: 0.0,height: 0.0}
    options := draw.text_options(15.0)
    first := "Complete one action"
    second := "Enable action button"
    mut model := Model{button: widgets.button(&identity,button_id,&empty) or >>,
        checkbox: widgets.checkbox(&identity,check_id,&empty,true) or >>,
        progress: widgets.progress(&empty,0.0) or >>,
        scroll: widgets.scroll(&identity,scroll_id,&empty,widgets.axis_vertical,760.0) or >>,
        button_label: context.layout_text(&first,&options) or >>,
        checkbox_label: context.layout_text(&second,&options) or >>,
        theme: widgets.default_theme(),context: context,count: 0,header_id: header,button_id: button_id,check_id: check_id,progress_id: progress_id,scroll_id: scroll_id,row_ids: rows}
    mut width := 700.0
    mut height := 600.0
    while true {
        constraints := layout.tight(width,height) or >>
        submitted := widgets.present(mut &view,&constraints,mut &model) or >>
        if submitted { model.acknowledge() }
        event := demo.next(ech)
        if event.kind == ui.ev_exit { <- }
        if event.window_id != identity.window_id { -> }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_canvas_metrics {
            metrics := ui.metrics_of(&event) or >>
            if metrics.width > 0.0 && metrics.height > 0.0 { width = metrics.width
                height = metrics.height }
        }
        actions := widgets.dispatch_event(mut &view,&event,mut &model) or >>
        for action in actions {
            if action.kind == widgets.action_activate {
                model.advance() or >>
                view.mark_paint(header) or >>
                view.mark_paint(progress_id) or >>
            }
            if action.kind == widgets.action_change {
                enabled := model.checkbox.value()
                model.enable_button(enabled)
                view.set_input(button_id,enabled,enabled) or >>
                view.mark_paint(button_id) or >>
            }
            if action.kind == widgets.action_scroll { view.set_scroll(scroll_id,0.0,action.value) or >> }
        }
        if event.kind == ui.ev_canvas_closed { <- }
        if ui.is_input_event(event.kind) {
            input := ui.input_of(&event) or >>
            if input.kind == ui.ev_key_down && input.key == ui.key_escape { demo.quit() or >> }
        }
        stats := view.stats()
        if stats.recorded_boundaries > 0 || stats.reused_boundaries > 0 {
            println("View: " + str(stats.recorded_boundaries) + " recorded / " + str(stats.reused_boundaries) + " reused boundaries")
        }
    }
    view.dispose()
    // Native close revokes resources; only release user handles while live.
    if demo.alive(&identity) {
        label := mut &model.button_label
        label.dispose() or >>
        check_label := mut &model.checkbox_label
        check_label.dispose() or >>
    }
    << 0
}
fn worker(ech chan ui.Event) { scene(ech) or (err) { demo.fail(err.msg())
    << } }
fn launch() int {
    mut app := demo.app("Xu UI / Retained Controls",700,600) or >>
    xu worker(app.event_ch)
    << app.run() or >>
}
fn main() int { << launch() or (err) { eprintln(err.msg())
    << 1 } }

struct Breadcrumbs

XuAPI
pub struct Breadcrumbs {

Purpose

The last item is the current location; earlier items activate navigation. Long paths are clipped consistently with hit testing, not silently wrapped.

Paint an image icon and label using styles. The icon preserves aspect ratio; the caller owns the Image.

Returns

0 on success; throws on failure

struct ColorPicker

XuAPI
pub struct ColorPicker {

}

Purpose

Inline RGBA picker: four quantized 0..255 channels and a checkerboard alpha preview. Up/Down selects a channel; Left/Right, Home/End and Page keys adjust it. Each channel is also a draggable Slider. This is deliberately a small RGB editor rather than an HSV spectrum, OS eyedropper or color-profile tool.

Parameters

none

color_picker

struct ComboBox

XuAPI
pub struct ComboBox {

}

Purpose

Editable choice field. Text is owned by Editor and travels through the native TextInput revision/IME protocol, including composition, clipboard and Unicode. Call event for native events and place_combo_box after each View layout. route receives node-local input; input receives canvas coordinates, which lets the scene intercept an open popup before dispatching an outside press. paint_styled paints locally like Editor; paint_popup_styled is canvas-space.

Parameters

none

combo_box

XuAPI
pub fn combo_box(context &draw.Context,identity &ui.CanvasEvent,id int,bounds &layout.Rect,options &[]string,value &string) ComboBox

Purpose

Combo box. Throws on failure.

context (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &draw.Context)
identity &ui.CanvasEvent
identity (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &ui.CanvasEvent)
id int
id (int)
bounds &layout.Rect
bounds (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &layout.Rect)

options (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &[]string)

struct Editor

struct Modal

XuAPI
pub struct Modal {

}

Purpose

Modal owns the keyboard focus order, while the host owns the actual child controls. Use blocks_input as the dispatch gate, including the closing fade. Cancel background View input before open (cancel_input) to release capture and native editor focus. Apply focused_id after open/input, and restore take_restore_focus after tick reports the closing fade finished. Never dispatch to background while blocks_input first call input, honor consumed even when that input closes the modal, then dispatch only to modal children if allows_content.

Parameters

none

modal

XuAPI
pub fn modal(identity &ui.CanvasEvent,id int,bounds &layout.Rect,viewport &layout.Rect) Modal

Purpose

modal; returns Modal. Throws on failure.

Parameters

identity &ui.CanvasEvent
identity (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &ui.CanvasEvent)
id int
id (int)
bounds &layout.Rect
bounds (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &layout.Rect)
viewport &layout.Rect
viewport (borrowed; caller keeps it; type &layout.Rect)

Returns

Modal — a value of this type

struct Pagination

XuAPI
pub struct Pagination {

}

Purpose

Zero based page state. At most seven page numbers are materialized; callers retain ownership of the actual paged data. Zero pages has current == -1.

Parameters

none

    fn layout(&mut Screen) int
    fn update(&mut Screen,Action) int
}

The callback owns business state only. Screen owns dispatch, rendering, native resources and cleanup, including errors from either callback.

struct StatePaint

XuAPI
pub struct StatePaint {
    pub when int
    pub unless int
    pub fields int
    pub background draw.Color
    pub foreground draw.Color
    pub border draw.Color
    pub focus draw.Color
    pub selection draw.Color
    pub selection_text draw.Color
    pub placeholder draw.Color
    pub icon_opacity f64
}

Purpose

Selective combined-state override. Matches all when bits and no unless bits; rules apply in array order, with later overrides winning.

Parameters

when int
required state bits, 0–127
unless int
excluded state bits; cannot overlap when
fields int
paint_* mask, 0–255; only selected fields change
background draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
foreground draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
border draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
focus draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
selection draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
selection_text draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
placeholder draw.Color
field (draw.Color)
icon_opacity f64
absolute resolved opacity, 0–1

state_paint

XuAPI
pub fn state_paint(when int) StatePaint

Purpose

Create a rule with when and fields=0. Select paint_* bits to activate the corresponding overrides.

Parameters

when int
required combined-state bits

Returns

a rule ready for customization

See usage example

struct FontStyle

XuAPI
pub struct FontStyle {
    pub family string
    pub size f64
    pub weight int
    pub italic bool
    pub line_height f64
}

Purpose

Font configuration. Empty family selects the system font; size and line_height use logical pixels.

Parameters

family string
UTF-8 family, at most 256 bytes without NUL; empty selects system font
size f64
base size, 1–512; scaled size must remain in range
weight int
weight, 100–900
italic bool
italic face
line_height f64
0 selects default; explicit value must be at least size and at most 1024

font_style

XuAPI
pub fn font_style(size f64) FontStyle

Purpose

Font style.

Parameters

size f64
size (f64)

Returns

FontStyle — a value of this type

struct Palette

XuAPI
pub struct Palette {
    pub normal draw.Color
    pub hovered draw.Color
    pub pressed draw.Color
    pub disabled draw.Color
    pub foreground draw.Color
    pub muted draw.Color
    pub accent draw.Color
    pub on_accent draw.Color
    pub focus draw.Color
    pub border draw.Color
    pub invalid draw.Color
    pub invalid_fill draw.Color
    pub read_only draw.Color
    pub selection draw.Color
    pub selection_text draw.Color
    pub placeholder draw.Color
}

Purpose

Base state palette. Background precedence: disabled, invalid, read-only, selected, pressed, hovered, normal.

Parameters

normal draw.Color
normal background
hovered draw.Color
hover background
pressed draw.Color
pressed background
disabled draw.Color
disabled background
foreground draw.Color
normal foreground
muted draw.Color
disabled foreground
accent draw.Color
selected background and progress indicator
on_accent draw.Color
selected foreground
focus draw.Color
focus ring
border draw.Color
normal border
invalid draw.Color
invalid border and progress indicator
invalid_fill draw.Color
invalid background
read_only draw.Color
read-only background
selection draw.Color
text selection background
selection_text draw.Color
text selection foreground
placeholder draw.Color
placeholder text

struct ControlStyle

XuAPI
pub struct ControlStyle {
    pub palette Palette
    pub font FontStyle
    pub inherit_font bool
    pub padding layout.Insets
    pub radius f64
    pub border_width f64
    pub focus_width f64
    pub focus_inset f64
    pub min_width f64
    pub min_height f64
    pub gap f64
    pub indicator_size f64
    pub mark_width f64
    pub caret_width f64
    pub preedit_width f64
    pub shadow_x f64
    pub shadow_y f64
    pub shadow_spread f64
    pub shadow_blur f64
    pub align_x f64
    pub align_y f64
    pub icon_size f64
    pub icon_placement int
    pub icon_opacity f64
    pub disabled_icon_opacity f64
    pub shadow draw.Color
    pub states []StatePaint
}

Purpose

Control colors, typography and geometry. Metrics are finite and nonnegative; offsets may be negative. clone deep-copies the rule array.

Parameters

palette Palette
base state colors
font FontStyle
local control font
inherit_font bool
true inherits Styles.font; false uses the local font
padding layout.Insets
content padding scaled by density
radius f64
corner radius scaled by density
border_width f64
border width scaled by density
focus_width f64
focus-ring width; 0 hides it
focus_inset f64
focus-ring inset
min_width f64
minimum logical width
min_height f64
minimum logical height; minimum thumb length for Scroll
gap f64
spacing between text, icons or indicators
indicator_size f64
checkbox indicator size or scrollbar thickness
mark_width f64
checkbox mark stroke width
caret_width f64
editor caret width
preedit_width f64
preedit underline width
shadow_x f64
horizontal shadow offset
shadow_y f64
vertical shadow offset
shadow_spread f64
shadow spread radius
shadow_blur f64
blur radius for the at-most-eight-layer approximation
align_x f64
horizontal alignment, 0–1
align_y f64
vertical alignment, 0–1
icon_size f64
image icon square size; 0 removes icon spacing
icon_placement int
icon_leading / trailing / above / below
icon_opacity f64
icon opacity, 0–1
disabled_icon_opacity f64
additional disabled opacity multiplier, 0–1
shadow draw.Color
shadow color; transparent disables shadow
states []StatePaint
up to 32 rules; later matching field overrides win

See usage example

struct Styles

XuAPI
pub struct Styles {
    pub font FontStyle
    pub text_scale f64
    pub density f64
    pub background draw.Color
    pub button ControlStyle
    pub checkbox ControlStyle
    pub input ControlStyle
    pub text_area ControlStyle
    pub progress ControlStyle
    pub scroll ControlStyle
}

Purpose

Complete control theme with independent text scaling and geometry density. Start with a factory, customize and validate.

Parameters

font FontStyle
global font for controls with inherit_font=true
text_scale f64
text scale, 0.5–4.0
density f64
geometry scale, 0.5–4.0; does not scale text
background draw.Color
View background; must be opaque when presenting
button ControlStyle
button style
checkbox ControlStyle
checkbox style
input ControlStyle
single-line editor style
text_area ControlStyle
multiline editor style
progress ControlStyle
progress style
scroll ControlStyle
scrollbar style

See usage example

struct Table

XuAPI
pub struct Table {

}

Purpose

A bounded single-line, equal-width column table: 1..16 columns and at most 768 cells (512 rows). Sorting emits action_activate; the caller owns order. Only visible rows are shaped, and their cache slots are reused on scroll.

struct Toast

XuAPI
pub struct Toast {

}

Purpose

One toast, with host-owned text and stacking. Lifetime zero is persistent. Hover/focus pauses expiry. tick/next_tick are explicit; idle toasts do no work.

struct Tooltip

}

struct TreeView

XuAPI
pub struct TreeView {

}

Purpose

Up to 512 nodes, depth <= 32. add(-1, label) appends a root; other parents must be an earlier node index. Indices stay stable until clear. Children are shown in insertion order, even when they are appended after siblings.

Parameters

none

struct View

XuAPI
pub struct View {

}

Purpose

One retained scene per canvas. The application keeps its control/model state this owner keeps stable topology, measured geometry and vector paint caches. Paint callbacks receive node-local bounds, in the same logical coordinate system returned by local_input. They must not mutate the View or topology.

Parameters

none

See usage example

struct@ StyledPainter

XuAPI
pub struct@ StyledPainter {
    fn measure_styled(int,&Styles) layout.Size
    fn paint_styled(int,&layout.Rect,&Styles,&mut draw.Frame) bool
    fn overlay_styled(int,&layout.Rect,&Styles,&mut draw.Frame) bool
}

Style-aware retained callbacks. Borrow the effective theme and paint in node-local coordinates. Do not mutate View or topology in callbacks. Overlay runs after children.
